| dret-cli/Cargo.toml | Crate manifest; deps: clap 4, dret-record, dret-replay, dret-trace, dret-gdb | | dret-cli/src/main.rs | Entry point; clap subcommand dispatch | | dret-cli/src/cmd_record.rs | dret record [--output DIR] -- <prog> [args]: build RecordConfig, run RecordSession | | dret-cli/src/cmd_replay.rs | dret replay [--trace DIR] [--gdb PORT]: build ReplayConfig, run ReplaySession, optionally start GdbServer | | dret-cli/src/cmd_dump.rs | dret dump [--trace DIR] [--from E] [--to E]: pretty-print TraceFrames as text | | dret-cli/src/cmd_pack.rs | dret pack [--trace DIR] [--output FILE]: embed mapped binaries + build-ids; compress data.log | | dret-cli/src/cmd_ps.rs | dret ps [--trace DIR]: tabulate tasks, tick ranges, checkpoint event IDs |

Tests, Corpus, Fuzz, Benchmarks

| tests/integration/record_replay.rs | End-to-end record hello_world / ls / cat; replay; assert zero divergence | | tests/integration/divergence_inject.rs | Mutate a register mid-replay; assert DivergenceReport fires at correct EventId | | tests/integration/multithreaded.rs | Record/replay pthreads mutex contention (threads_mutex.c); verify tick-for-tick match | | tests/integration/signals.rs | SIGALRM async + SIGSEGV sync record/replay; verify exact signo + tick + si_code | | tests/integration/checkpoint_seek.rs | Seek to mid-trace Ticks; verify RecordedRegs matches trace at that EventId | | tests/integration/reverse_debug.rs | GDB reverse-step / reverse-continue via dret-gdb; verify rip progression | | tests/integration/vdso_rdtsc.rs | Record/replay program using rdtsc and gettimeofday; assert zero divergence | | tests/corpus/hello_world.c | Minimal C: write + exit; primary conformance subject | | tests/corpus/threads_mutex.c | Pthreads with mutex contention + condition variable; multi-thread replay subject | | tests/corpus/signal_storm.c | Mixed SIGALRM (async) + deliberate SIGSEGV (sync); signal capture subject | | tests/corpus/rdtsc_cpuid.c | Calls rdtsc, rdtscp, cpuid in a loop; nondeterministic instruction subject | | tests/corpus/mmap_heavy.c | Repeated mmap/munmap/mprotect; AddressSpace tracking subject | | tests/fuzz/fuzz_trace_reader.rs | cargo-fuzz target: arbitrary bytes into TraceReader; assert no panic or UB | | tests/fuzz/fuzz_syscall_table.rs | cargo-fuzz target: arbitrary nr + regs into SyscallSpec decoder; assert no panic | | benchmarks/record_overhead.rs | criterion: dret-record wall-time overhead vs untraced execution on hello_world | | benchmarks/replay_throughput.rs | criterion: events/sec during dense trace replay | | benchmarks/checkpoint_latency.rs | criterion: checkpoint write + restore round-trip latency at 50MB heap |

Scripts and Documentation

| scripts/gen_syscall_table.py | Generate dret-syscall/src/table.rs from kernel unistd_64.h + hand annotations | | scripts/check_trace_integrity.sh | CRC-check all frames in a trace directory; report first corrupt frame | | scripts/run_conformance.sh | Record + replay all corpus programs; diff divergence counts | | docs/trace_format.md | Wire specification: events.log frame layout, data.log framing, mmap.log record types, header fields | | docs/rsp_subset.md | Documents the RSP command subset implemented by dret-gdb with packet examples | | docs/porting.md | Prerequisites for aarch64 port: PMU counter selection, signal ABI differences, TLS register | | docs/design_decisions.md | ADR log: rationale for RCB as clock, PTRACE_SYSEMU choice, single logical CPU approach, trace format versioning | | docs/threat_model.md | Security analysis: tracee escape vectors, untrusted trace input, seccomp filter bypass |

Appendix B — Milestone Summary

Total: approximately 30 weeks engineering. Phase 0 (bootstrap) is week 0 (0.5 wk). Phases 1–14 sum to ~29.5 weeks. Assumes one full-time senior engineer; a team of two halves calendar time without changing phase order (phases 1–6 are strictly serial; phases 7–9 could partially parallelize in a team context).

Phase Exit-Criteria Detail

Each phase exit criteria column states the minimum observable condition for the phase to be considered complete. Exit criteria are verified by automated tests in the integration suite unless marked (manual).

Phase Deliverable Duration Key Risks Exit Criteria
0 — Bootstrap Repo scaffold, CI green, all 12 crate stubs compile, deny.toml clean 0.5 wk CI environment: exact kernel version for ptrace/perf_event; capset permissions cargo build --workspace passes on x86-64 Linux >= 5.4; CI green
1 — ptrace supervisor skeleton Attach/spawn under PTRACE_TRACEME; SETOPTIONS (EXITKILL, TRACESYSGOOD, TRACECLONE/FORK/EXEC); waitpid demux; single-step; clean detach 1.5 wk Kernel ptrace option differences across distros; zombie process leak on supervisor crash Tracee runs hello_world to exit under supervisor; EXITKILL kills tracee on supervisor SIGKILL
2 — syscall interception + ABI tables PTRACE_SYSCALL entry/exit pairs; SyscallSpec table; arg decode from regs; mem-effect classification; record-passthrough (real syscall + capture) 2 wk Pointer-arg len-pairing edge cases: iovec array, sockaddr+len, msghdr; 64-bit only scope All syscalls in corpus decoded without panic; arg + ret + errno logged correctly for read/write/mmap/clone
3 — trace container format events.log framed records; data.log variable payloads; mmap.log; ckpt/ index; versioned header; CRC32 per frame; TraceWriter/Reader; forward-compat tag skip 1.5 wk CRC performance on large data.log payloads; forward-compat tag schema freeze Round-trip write/read of 10k frames is bit-exact; unknown-tag skip test passes without data loss
4 — deterministic clock (RCB ticks) perf_event_open per task (PERF_COUNT_HW_BRANCH_INSTRUCTIONS); ticks read at every stop; skid correction via single-step 2 wk PMU skid variance across Intel microarchitectures and AMD; perf_event_paranoid in CI Ticks monotone across 10k-event trace; measured skid <= 5 branches on target hardware; reproducible between two record runs of same binary
5 — memory capture /proc/pid/mem pread/pwrite for mem_effects; COW page snapshot; page-diff delta; AddressSpace tracking via mmap/munmap/brk interception 2 wk TOCTOU window between syscall return and mem snapshot; large heap checkpoint size Checkpoint restores AddressSpace byte-exact on 50MB heap; all recorded mem_effects match kernel writes
6 — single-threaded replay engine PTRACE_SYSEMU; inject ret + errno + mem_effects; validate RecordedRegs + Ticks per event; deterministic mmap address enforcement 2 wk PTRACE_SYSEMU availability on kernel < 5.11; mmap address layout ASLR nondeterminism hello_world replays 100 consecutive runs with zero divergence; all I4 / I5 invariants verified by test
7 — deterministic user-space scheduler sched_setaffinity(1 CPU); tick-budget preemption; SchedSwitch record/replay; SchedPoint determinism 2 wk perf_event overflow interrupt latency jitter; sched_setaffinity permission in container CI Single-threaded replay unaffected; 2-thread program produces identical SchedSwitch sequence across 50 replay runs
8 — multi-threaded record + replay clone/clone3 interception; per-thread TaskId + tick counter + RecordedRegs; futex ordering serialization; TLS fs_base capture/restore 3 wk Futex wake-ordering correctness under high contention; fs_base restore on context-switch race threads_mutex corpus replays with zero divergence 50 consecutive times; I6 (one runnable task) verified per-event
9 — signal capture + deterministic delivery Async vs sync signal classification; delivery tick pinning; si_info capture; PTRACE_SETSIGINFO replay at exact tick 2 wk Async signal arriving during a ptrace stop (queued vs delivered); re-entrant signal delivery to multiple threads signal_storm corpus: every replayed signal matches recorded signo + delivery_ticks + si_code exactly
10 — nondeterministic instruction handling rdtsc/rdtscp/cpuid trap via seccomp-BPF + inject recorded values; vsyscall/vDSO gettimeofday patch; CPUID feature masking 2 wk CR4.TSD privilege level for rdtsc trap; vDSO layout changes across kernel versions rdtsc_cpuid corpus and gettimeofday-using programs replay with zero divergence; CPUID values match on different CPU model
11 — checkpointing + fast seek Periodic checkpoint write; seek(TaskId, Ticks) via checkpoint restore + replay-forward; reverse-continue skeleton 2 wk fd-table restoration for pipes and sockets; checkpoint size growth with dirty working set Seek to arbitrary mid-trace Ticks within 2s on 1M-event trace; register state exact at target; checkpoint round-trip test
12 — divergence detection Per-event hash (regs + ticks + page hashes); DivergenceReport; bisect tooling; fail-closed abort 1.5 wk False positives from non-deterministic page hash ordering; per-event hashing performance overhead Injected register mutation detected at correct EventId; zero false positives on 10 clean replays; bisect converges in <= log2(N) re-runs
13 — gdbserver + reverse execution RSP stub over TCP; g/G/m/M/c/s; reverse-step / reverse-continue via checkpoint rewind; write watchpoints 2.5 wk GDB version RSP dialect differences; watchpoint false-negatives on write-through pages gdb attaches and reverse-steps to correct prior rip; watchpoint fires on recorded write address; no RSP protocol errors on standard GDB 12+
14 — trace packing, hardening, conformance dret pack (embed binaries + build-ids; compress data.log); batch ptrace perf; fuzz corpus (1h); 1000-run soak record/replay equivalence 2 wk Compression vs random-seek performance tradeoff; fuzz-discovered TraceReader edge cases; batch ptrace PTRACE_PEEKDATA throughput ceiling Packed trace self-contained on clean machine without original binary; 1000-run soak zero divergence; fuzz 1h no crash/UB
Total Full record/replay system with reverse debugging, gdbserver, packing ~30 wk Multi-thread futex ordering; skid correction across Intel/AMD; vDSO portability All 14 phase exit criteria green; CI passes; conformance corpus 100% replay-clean
